Abstract: We present a method to obtain the proven exact 2-component (X2C) method, normally applied to Gaussian-type fixed Hilbert bases, in an adaptative non-uniform grid basis of the Hilbert space, namely Multiwavelets. The X2C method manages to block-diagonalise the Dirac Hamiltonian restricted to the subspace of the basis, in an energy-independent manner. The method takes inspiration from the success of the atomic mean-field X2C method on the Gaussian bases to represent the coupling operator as a projector onto the small components of the eigenstates of the constituent atoms of the system.

</details>


### [75] [Quantum Darwinism through apparatus-mediated environmental recording](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07568)
*Tran P. H Don,Lan Nguyen Tran,Le Bin Ho*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文用精确可解的量子线路模型证明：即便系统与环境无直接相互作用，通过中间测量装置也能建立环境冗余记录，从而扩展了量子达尔文主义的适用范围，并厘清了噪声位置对记录可靠性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子达尔文主义通常假设系统与环境直接相互作用产生冗余记录，但实际中记录往往经由中间测量装置间接建立。论文旨在检验这种间接测量能否同样产生客观性所需的冗余信息，并考察噪声在何处作用会影响记录的可靠性。

Method: 采用量子线路模型的精确解析解，对比"系统监测"（环境与系统直接耦合）与"装置监测"（环境只与装置耦合）两种方案。分析编码过程中系统–装置关联、系统–环境关联的此消彼长，并以互信息、discord、可获取的指针信息与冗余为指标进行比较。进一步引入比特翻转噪声与非相干（退相位）噪声，考察噪声作用于装置或环境不同位置时的效果。

Result: 无噪声时两种模型给出完全相同的可获取指针信息与冗余，差异仅体现在互信息中由discord贡献的部分。装置上的比特翻转会引入连续记录共享的错误，即使获取完整环境也无法完全恢复信息；而编码后环境量子比特上的独立噪声反而可由更大的环境片段来缓解。指针基下的退相位不改变记录信息。

Conclusion: 结果表明量子达尔文主义可推广到间接测量情形，环境记录不必依赖系统–环境直接相互作用；记录的可靠性取决于噪声作用的位置，装置噪声具有连带损害而环境独立噪声可通过片段冗余抑制。这为实验与理论中判断环境记录的客观性提供了依据。

Abstract: Quantum Darwinism explains classical objectivity through redundant environmental records of a system's pointer states. We show that these records can be established through an intermediate measurement apparatus without direct system--environment interactions. Using exact analytical solutions of quantum circuit models, we compare system monitoring with apparatus monitoring, where the environment interacts only with the apparatus. During encoding, system--apparatus correlations decrease as system--environment correlations grow. Without noise, both models yield identical accessible pointer information and redundancy, while the discord accounts for their different mutual information. The reliability of these records depends on where noise acts. Apparatus bit flips introduce errors shared by successive records, limiting the information recoverable even from the complete environment. In contrast, larger fragments can mitigate independent noise on environment qubits after encoding. Dephasing in the pointer basis leaves the record information unchanged in the model considered. These results extend the description of quantum Darwinism to indirect measurements and clarify the conditions for reliable environmental records.

</details>

Motivation: 有限亚波长原子链的亚辐射态是光子存储的重要资源，但其制备与控制实验上困难。传统激光驱动方法受限于亚辐射流形内模式高度简并、近乎形成连续带，难以选择性激发给定亚辐射态。

Method: 将原子与沿金属薄膜界面传播的表面等离激元波耦合，通过调节原子链与表面的距离来控制耦合，从而激发给定的亚辐射态。

Abstract: Subradiant states of a finite subwavelength atomic chain in free space offer a powerful resource for photon storage. Yet their preparation and control remain challenging experimentally. A standard technique is to drive the atoms by a laser and observe the florescence light of the long-lived subradiant modes of excitation after the laser is switched off. However, these modes are highly degenerate and form an almost continuous band in the subradiant manifold. Here we propose a new method of exciting a given subradiant state by coupling atoms to a surface plasmon wave propagating along the interface with a metallic film. By controlling the chain/surface distance we may load, store and release photons in the atoms, making them a promising tool for future quantum memories.

</details>

Candidate-only and incremental projection scores provide local criteria for ranking insertions. A broader TFIM routing ensemble exhibits routing-dependent near-stagnation. Together, these results connect representational capacity, local accessibility, and same-point repair through a common discrete routing description. An architecture need not remain fixed during training: task-dependent geometric information can guide the opening of new descent directions while preserving the learned circuit at insertion. This provides a concrete local approach to a broader problem: how useful quantum circuits can be discovered from the information a task supplies.

Abstract: Fragment molecular orbital (FMO) quantum chemistry offers a natural route to parallel quantum computing: large molecular calculations can be decomposed into smaller quantum subproblems that can, in principle, run concurrently. Their practical benefit on real quantum hardware, however, has remained unclear. We demonstrate parallel FMO calculations on three room-temperature diamond-based 'Quoll' quantum computers installed on-site at the Oak Ridge Leadership Computing Facility. Helium clusters (He$_{n}$, $n=2,4,8,10,14$) served as a testbed for total energy calculations using the Quantum Wave Function Sampler (QWFS), a selected configuration interaction (SCI) approach. We compare two execution modes: synchronous shot-parallel execution, in which quantum processors jointly sample QWFS circuits and combine their SPAM-corrected counts into a single probability distribution; and asynchronous fragment-parallel execution, in which processors independently evaluate FMO fragments using device-specific optimized circuits. Both modes increase quantum sampling throughput without systematic loss of accuracy. The three-QPU shot-parallel workflow reaches a parallel efficiency of $93.60\%$, while the fragment-parallel workflow achieves a measured efficiency of $75.01\%$, increasing to $95.17\%$ when excluding the non-useful idle time associated with non-interruptible submitted jobs. In both cases, the assembled FMO-QWFS energies remain chemically accurate compared to the classical ideal. These results provide a proof-of-concept demonstration that parallel quantum execution can improve the practical throughput of quantum chemistry workflows on prototype hardware, motivating the development of future HPC environments with many fault-tolerant quantum processors operating as a distributed resource for molecular and materials simulation.

Abstract: Quantum telescopy, proposed by Grottesman et al., uses spatially entangled single-photon states to modify HBT stellar interferometry, potentially surpassing Michelson interferometry in resolution without baseline limits. However, finite source bandwidth, non-unit efficiency, detector response times, and celestial light bandwidth all affect its effectiveness. In this work, we consider a spatially entangled singe-photon state generated by beam-splitting a practical anti-bunched photon source from resonance fluorescence, which is a common method of generating single-photon states, and evaluate the signal level in quantum telescopy with inclusion of imperfection of detection system and finite bandwidth of celestial light. Here, given the incoming light, we optimize the parameters of the anti-bunched photon source and the detector system for the best signal-to-noise ratio (SNR), which can match that of the Michelson method in the ideal case but is always smaller in the realistic case. Alternatively, weak coherent states from heavily attenuated lasers can replace the entangled single-photon state but suffer low signal level, similar to HBT method. We find that the SNR with a realistic anti-bunched source for producing the reference fields can still beat the SNR of the coherent state scheme in the weak incoming field limit. The optimized parameters for the anti-bunched source and detection system will be a guide for future experimental endeavor.

</details>

Abstract: A ruler reads an unknown length against a calibrated scale. The quantum data ruler reads a matrix element of an unknown state against the signal of a known reference, from their ratio. The readout is unbiased under dephasing and amplitude damping, and recovers the coherent ergotropy of a quantum battery that two-point measurement erases. The structure survives the classical limit as action--angle Fourier modes.

</details>

Abstract: We study how a parity--time-symmetric photonic environment changes the charging of Dicke and Tavis--Cummings quantum batteries. For atoms prepared in the collective ground state the excitation number grows as the square of the charging time, with a coefficient set by the initial mean photon number; the counter-rotating terms of the Dicke model add one to four times that number. Conservation of the total excitation number turns this into an exact ceiling on the stored Tavis--Cummings energy, the transition energy times the smaller of the photon and atom numbers, met numerically to within two per cent, which the Dicke terms overshoot by a few per cent at ultrastrong coupling. We then couple a battery to a lossy--gain pair, keeping the parity--time symmetry intact by putting an identical battery in each cavity; the closed moment system puts the exceptional point at the loss rate. Below it the gain cavity fills and the gain-side battery charges while the lossy-side one stays empty; above it photons are shuttled away and dissipated. Solving the full Lindblad equation with batched Monte--Carlo wave functions, we find that the gain-side battery gains thirty-nine, twenty-six and twenty-eight per cent in stored energy, power and ergotropy in the broken phase and loses sixty-five, thirty-five and sixty-five per cent deep in the unbroken phase, where the lossy-side battery overtakes it. Restoring the counter-rotating terms leaves all of this unchanged within the statistical error; what they add is a reactive oscillation at twice the transition frequency that does no net work but nearly doubles the peak of the instantaneous power. The extra energy is of lower quality, the recoverable fraction dropping from about ninety-eight to ninety per cent. The optimum sits on the broken side, close to but not at the exceptional point.

Abstract: We study divisibility of finite-dimensional quantum dynamics in the Heisenberg picture when the dynamical maps need not be invertible. Heisenberg divisible dynamics determines unique reduced propagators on the accessible operator systems, and Heisenberg CP-divisibility is shown to be equivalent to an operator-norm monotonicity. Although each reduced propagator admits a unital completely positive extension, independently chosen extensions need not satisfy a composition law. Generalized inverses restore this law algebraically, but do not, in general, preserve complete positivity. We characterize the resulting obstruction and give a sufficient construction based on unital completely positive projections onto the accessible operator systems. It is shown that for qubit dynamics Heisenberg CP-divisibility always guarantees a completely positive generalized-inverse propagator. Finally, we analyze time-local generators at singular times and identify the associated gauge freedom on dynamically inaccessible directions.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 作者针对给定协议建立一个解析模型，用于估计宣告阶段的退相干对最终纠缠率的影响，并分析中继器数量变化时纠缠生成增益与存储退相干累积效应之间的相互竞争。

Result: 分析表明存在两种相互竞争的现象：中继器数量增加会提升纠缠生成，但同时存储退相干对最终纠缠率的累积负面影响也增大，因此存在一个最优的中继器数量。并且模型可量化宣告退相干的影响。

Conclusion: 该研究为未来量子网络设计提供了依据，尤其可指导中继器间距与纠缠交换时序的选取，以在生成增益与退相干损耗之间取得最优平衡。

Abstract: First-generation quantum repeaters are essential in distributing entanglement over long distances since they could mitigate losses in optical fiber. Repeaters split the total distance into shorter, easier to generate links. Then, entanglement swapping establishes long range entanglement by performing local measurements at the repeaters and heralding their results. In this work, we develop an analytical model that estimates the impact of decoherence during heralding for a given protocol. Furthermore, we identify a competition between two phenomena: as repeater count increases, so does entanglement generation, but the additive impact of memory decoherence on final entanglement rate increases too. Therefore, we find that an optimal number of repeaters exists. These findings could lead the design of future quantum networks, both in the inter-repeater distance and swapping timings.

</details>

Abstract: The possibility of generating light in a squeezed quantum state during saturated light amplification in a semiconductor amplifier is theoretically analyzed. Due to four-wave mixing process during amplification, weak components of the spontaneous emission of the medium with symmetric frequency shifts relative to the strong amplified wave acquire the quantum properties of two-mode squeezed light. The squeezing of quadrature components occurs in the range of frequency shifts of several GHz ($\sim$ the inverse relaxation time of the charge carrier density) and can reach 10 dB or more under optimal conditions. The formation of a two-mode squeezing phase facilitates the observation of the squeezing effect, in which a strong wave, playing the role of a local wave, exhibits quadrature noise suppression without additional homodyne detection.

</details>


### [86] [Cyclic triorthogonal codes in prime dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08012)
*Shiroman Prakash*

TL;DR: 本文研究素数维度下的循环三正交码，证明横向三阶对角门可实现逻辑非Clifford门，并通过谱支撑集上的和集条件实现穷举搜索，发现了在开销指数和蒸馏阈值上优于Reed-Muller与Reed-Solomon码的量子码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究循环三正交码是为了在素数维度$p$下通过横向三阶对角门实现逻辑非Clifford门，从而获得在开销指数与蒸馏阈值上优于现有Reed-Muller和Reed-Solomon码的量子纠错码，并系统刻画这类码的结构与分类。

Method: 对奇素数$p$采用不要求自正交的三正交定义；对长度与$p$互素的循环码，将三正交性约化为谱支撑集上的和集条件，从而使穷举搜索可行；对两类自然构造，枚举$p=3$、长度至121以及$p=5,7$、长度至96的所有极大此类码；对量子比特情形证明奇数长度循环码三偶当且仅当经典三正交，并确定长度至763的全部此类码。

Result: 穷举搜索得到全部极大循环三正交码；对量子比特给出三偶循环码的完整描述并确定长度至763的所有码；对qudit发现如$[[19,1,3]]_3$和$[[7,1,3]]_7$等码，在开销指数和蒸馏阈值两方面均优于最短的Reed-Muller与Reed-Solomon码，且后者是无限族$[[p,1,2\lfloor p/6\rfloor+1]]_p$的首个成员。

Conclusion: 循环三正交码在素数维度下可借助和集条件高效枚举，并给出优于现有构造的量子码；量子比特情形得到完整分类，qudit情形发现具有更好开销指数与蒸馏阈值的无限族码。

Abstract: We study cyclic triorthogonal codes in prime dimension $p$, on which a transversal third-level diagonal gate acts as a logical non-Clifford gate. For odd $p$, we use a definition of triorthogonality that does not require self-orthogonality. For cyclic codes of length coprime to $p$, triorthogonality reduces to a sumset condition on the spectral support, which makes exhaustive search feasible: for two natural constructions, we enumerate all maximal codes of this kind for $p=3$ up to length $121$ and $p=5,7$ up to length $96$. For qubits, we prove that a cyclic code of odd length is triply even if and only if it is classically triorthogonal, giving a complete description of triply-even cyclic codes, and we determine all such codes up to length $763$. For qudits, we find codes such as $[[19,1,3]]_3$ and $[[7,1,3]]_7$, which improve on the shortest Reed-Muller and Reed-Solomon codes in both overhead exponent and distillation threshold. The latter is the first member of an infinite family of $[[p,1,2\lfloor p/6\rfloor+1]]_p$ codes.

</details>


### [87] [Broadband Telecom Entanglement from an All-Fiber Type-0 Sagnac Source](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08025)

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 该全光纤源可支持超越经典极限的量子比特隐形传态，其宽带发射与光纤接口可用于通过多通道波分复用实现多用户纠缠分发。

Abstract: We demonstrate a compact, practical all-fiber source of non-degenerate telecom-band polarization-entangled photon pairs assembled from off-the-shelf components. The compact fiber-integrated architecture is designed to be affordable, straightforward to assemble and align, and compatible with existing optical-fiber infrastructure. Photon pairs are generated through type-0 spontaneous parametric down-conversion in a pigtailed periodically poled lithium-niobate waveguide embedded in a Sagnac interferometer. At 0.5 mW pump power, quantum-state tomography yields a Bell-state fidelity $F_{Φ^-}=0.850$, purity $P=0.836$, and concurrence $C=0.782$. A separate CHSH measurement gives $S=2.3301\pm0.0075$. The measured Bell-state fidelity is sufficient for the reconstructed polarization state to support qubit teleportation above the classical fidelity limit under ideal protocol operations. Analysis of the density matrix shows that approximately 96.7% of the population remains in the desired $|HH\rangle$-$|VV\rangle$ subspace, with a normalized coherence of approximately 0.86. A residual relative-phase offset indicates that local phase compensation could increase the Bell-state fidelity to approximately 0.8865. We further characterize the brightness--quality trade-off and obtain a corrected pair-rate slope of $(0.918\pm0.040)$ Mcps mW$^{-1}$. The broadband emission and fiber interfaces offer a route to multiuser entanglement distribution through off-the-shelf multichannel wavelength-division multiplexers with passbands matched to the signal and idler wavelengths.

</details>


### [88] [Memory Separations from a Collective-Spin Quantum Register](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08038)
*Jamal Slim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 一个仅由总自旋算符驱动的自旋1/2系统产生一种更新过程，其中一个事件间隔 n* 被禁止而其他所有间隔均可出现。任何经典隐马尔可夫模型至少需 1+√n* 个状态才能复现该过程，这种无界分离的唯一资源是一个相对相位 π。使用 K 个振幅时，相同干涉产生 K(K+1)/2 个衰变通道；在正权重与一般速率下，经典代价恰为 K(K+1)/2 个状态。两者均可由深度为三的电路实现，且参数数量不随寄存器规模增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究量子干涉（单一相对相位 π）相对于经典隐马尔可夫模型在生成随机事件序列时的表达能力优势，量化量子与经典模型之间的分离程度。

Method: 以仅由总自旋算符驱动的自旋1/2系统构造更新过程，通过振幅干涉（K 个振幅对应 K(K+1)/2 个衰变通道）实现特定事件间隔禁戒；并用深度为三、参数数不随寄存器增长的电路实现。

Result: 单个禁戒间隔 n* 导致经典隐马尔可夫模型至少需 1+√n* 个状态，分离无界；正权重与一般速率下经典代价精确为 K(K+1)/2 个状态；电路深度为三且参数量恒定。

Conclusion: 仅凭一个相对相位 π 的量子干涉即可产生经典隐马尔可夫模型无法高效复现的更新过程，且可由参数高效的浅层电路实现，表明量子模型在此类随机过程中具有本质的表达优势。

Abstract: A spin one-half driven only by total-spin operators generates a renewal process in which one interevent time $n^\ast$ is forbidden while all others occur. No classical hidden Markov model reproduces it with fewer than $1+\sqrt{n^\ast}$ states, an unbounded separation whose only resource is one relative phase of $π$. With $K$ amplitudes the same interference yields $K(K{+}1)/2$ decay channels, and for positive weights and generic rates the classical cost is exactly $K(K{+}1)/2$ states. Both are realized by a depth-three circuit whose parameter count does not grow with the register.

</details>


### [89] [Total Ordering of Resources: A Quantum-Classical Separation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08062)
*Jingsong Ao,Julio I. de Vicente,Alexander Streltsov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明了维度大于2的量子系统不存在能够对所有量子态进行完全排序的单一可转换性序关系，即量子态转换本质上是偏序而非全序；同时构造了任意规模的经典系统可实现完美排序，从而严格区分了量子与经典态转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子信息科学的核心目标之一是理解哪些物理态可以转换为其他态。最理想的框架是完全有序系统：每个态由单一数值排序，高排序态总能转换为低排序态，从而对态可转换性给出完整描述，使资源结构的刻画与比较极大简化。作者旨在探究这种简化序结构是否对量子系统成立。

Method: 通过证明（理论构造与不可能性证明）而非实验手段：对任意维度大于2的量子系统，证明不存在单一数值的完全排序能够刻画态可转换性；并构造任意规模、可实现完美排序的经典系统作为对照。

Result: 证明对任何维度大于2的量子系统，这种简单全序在本质上不可能存在；量子力学的基本性质保证总存在不可比较的量子态。相反，任意规模的经典系统都可被完美排序。

Conclusion: 量子态转换结构无法约化为单一数值的全序，必然包含不可比较态；而经典系统可以做到完全排序。这建立了量子与经典态转换之间严格而鲜明的分界，表明态不可比较性是量子信息独有的现象。

Abstract: A central goal in quantum information science is understanding which physical states can be converted into others. The ideal scenario would be a totally ordered system, where every state can be ranked by a single value, and a higher-ranked state can always be converted into a lower-ranked one. Such an ordering would provide a complete description of state convertibility, making the resource structure maximally simple to characterize and compare. We prove that such a simple ordering is fundamentally impossible for any quantum system with dimension larger than 2. The underlying properties of quantum mechanics guarantee that some states will always be incomparable. Furthermore, we show this is a uniquely quantum phenomenon by constructing classical systems of any size that can be perfectly ranked. This establishes a rigorous and striking divide between quantum and classical state transformations.

</details>


### [90] [Operational quasiprobability formulation of non-signaling in time for macrorealism violations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08146)
*Stefano Gherardini,Paolo Solinas*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出准非时序信号（QNSIT）概念，将非时序信号（NSIT）推广到准概率分布，并据此给出宏观实在论的准概率刻画MR_qp，证明MR_qp蕴含NSIT但反之不成立，且在两能级系统中两者等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NSIT作为检验宏观实在论的条件在某些情形下不够灵敏，无法捕捉由系统动力学产生或保留的隐藏叠加路径，因此需要一种更精细的准概率刻画。

Method: 引入准非时序信号QNSIT，要求准概率分布在中间时刻的边缘分布不受测量反作用扰动；据此定义宏观实在论的准概率条件MR_qp，要求两个QNSIT条件均满足且测量结果构成的准概率分布为正；并进行理论证明与两能级系统分析，提出基于量子非破坏测量的实验实现方案。

Result: 证明MR_qp蕴含NSIT，反之不成立，原因在于系统动力学产生或保留的隐藏叠加路径；对于两能级系统，MR_qp与NSIT条件重合。

Conclusion: MR_qp为检验宏观实在论提供了更灵敏的工具，并可通过量子非破坏测量方案在实验上实现。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce the concept of quasi non-signaling in time (QNSIT), which extends non-signaling in time (NSIT) to quasiprobability distributions. QNSIT requires that the marginals of the quasiprobability distribution under consideration at intermediate times remain unperturbed by the measurement back-action induced by a sequence of quantum measurements. Associated with QNSIT, we propose a quasiprobabilistic characterization of macrorealism, ${\rm MR}_{\rm qp}$, which requires that both the QNSIT conditions are satisfied and the quasiprobability distribution constructed from the measurement outcomes is positive. We prove that ${\rm MR}_{\rm qp}$ implies NSIT, whereas the converse does not hold because of hidden superposition paths generated or preserved by the system dynamics. We also prove that, for a two-level system, the ${\rm MR}_{\rm qp}$ and NSIT conditions coincide. ${\rm MR}_{\rm qp}$ therefore provides a more sensitive tool for testing macrorealism and can be implemented experimentally through a quantum non-demolition measurement scheme.

</details>


### [91] [The polarization behavior of a system of two interacting identical spins 1/2 in adiabatically varying magnetic field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08147)
*Yury Belousov,Dim Safin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在绝热表示下推导了非平稳系统的密度矩阵与Liouville方程，并以磁偶极耦合的两个自旋1/2粒子为例，研究伪qutrit纠缠态的自旋极化行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准相互作用表象在部分非平稳系统中不再适用，需要一种替代表示来处理此类量子系统的密度矩阵与动力学演化问题。

Method: 在绝热表示下推导非平稳系统的密度矩阵及对应Liouville方程；以缓慢变化外磁场中两个空间分离、经磁偶极-偶极相互作用的相同自旋1/2粒子为例进行说明；分析伪qutrit态在纠缠区域的自旋极化。

Result: 绝热表示可有效计算量子系统可观测量随时间的变化；纠缠态会显著改变这些可观测量的行为，并为其调控提供额外途径。

Conclusion: 绝热表示适用于标准相互作用表象失效的情形，且纠缠态的引入可显著影响并增强量子系统可观测量的可控性。

Abstract: We derive the density matrix of a nonstationary system in the adiabatic representation and obtain the corresponding Liouville equation. This representation is particularly suitable when the standard interaction representation is inapplicable. We illustrate the approach with two identical, spatially separated spin-1/2 particles coupled by a magnetic dipole-dipole interaction in a slowly varying external magnetic field. Specifically, we analyze the spin polarization of pseudo-qutrit states in regimes where the states become entangled. The application of the adiabatic representation to calculate the dependence of observed physical quantities on time for quantum systems is illustrated. Our results show that entangled states can significantly modify the behavior of these observables and provide additional opportunities for their control.

</details>


### [92] [Classical Verification of a Remote Quantum Processor via Degenerate Concordant Computations: An Application to Randomness Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08185)
*Bishal Kumar Das,Lakshya Priyadarshi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种基于简并协调系综的云量子处理器验证协议，使客户端每轮验证耗时从 O(2^n) 降至 O(n)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 租用云量子处理器的客户端难以判断收到的比特串是否真的来自量子设备。现有随机电路采样认证方法验证每个返回样本需客户端耗费 O(2^n) 时间，效率过低，因此需要一种客户端高效的验证方案。

Method: 基于简并协调系综构建协议：当协调态具有简并谱时，每个保持协调性的门分解为 G_t=U_tP_tB_tU_{t-1}^† ，与态对易的块因子 B_t 从系综中消去同时纠缠各纯分量。客户端持有秘密宇称，其余集为各层简并扇区，从而每层仅需一次内积即可构建电路并预测系综，秘密始终不离开客户端。输入均匀随机发送，客户端在自身记录中为每轮附加该输入在隐藏态中的特征值；再用输入-输出一致性检验与加权碰撞检验两项统计量，比较服务器样本与协调计算的非平坦输出分布。

Result: 在编译电路不泄露秘密的理想模型下证明了完备性、客户端效率与可靠性，并证明秘密必须保持隐藏（持有秘密的经典服务器可通过）。针对显式构造，证明高效模拟协调计算所用的单量子比特对称性检验在具有无偏边缘分布的非乘积输入上失败；并测得 Cable 与 Browne 的局部基查找器在测试的每一层均停机、传输分量达到最大 Schmidt 秩、电路非 Clifford、矩阵乘积欺骗者被拒绝。

Conclusion: 协议实现了每轮 O(n) 的客户端验证效率，优于现有 O(2^n) 方案，并在理想模型下具备完备性与可靠性。显式构造的可靠性仍以猜想形式给出，尚待完整证明。

Abstract: A client who rents a cloud quantum processor cannot easily tell whether the bitstrings it receives came from a quantum device. Certification by random circuit sampling runs on present hardware, but scoring each returned sample costs the client time $O(2^n)$. We give a protocol in which the client scores each round in time $O(n)$, built on degenerate concordant ensembles. When a concordant state has a degenerate spectrum, every concordance-preserving gate factorizes as $G_t=U_tP_tB_tU_{t-1}^\dagger$, and the block factor $B_t$, which commutes with the state, cancels from the ensemble while entangling each pure component. The client holds a secret parity whose cosets are the degenerate sectors of every layer, so that it builds the circuit and predicts the ensemble with one inner product per layer. The secret never leaves the client. Inputs are sent uniformly at random, and the client attaches to each round, in its own bookkeeping, the eigenvalue of that input in the hidden state; both verification statistics, an input--output agreement test and a weighted collision test, then compare the server's samples with the non-flat output distribution of the concordant computation. We prove completeness, client efficiency, and soundness in an ideal model in which the compiled circuit reveals nothing about the secret, and we show that the secret must stay hidden: a classical server that holds it passes. For the explicit construction we prove that the single-qubit symmetry test used in efficient simulations of concordant computation fails on non-product inputs with unbiased marginals, and we measure that the local-basis finder of Cable and Browne halts on every layer we tested, that the transmitted component saturates the maximal Schmidt rank, that the circuit is not Clifford, and that matrix-product spoofers are rejected. Soundness of the explicit construction is stated as a conjecture.

</details>


### [93] [Post-Selection-Free Quantum Automated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08219)
*Junkai Wang,Jin-Peng Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出无后选择量子自动学习算法，将训练路径组织为相干量子电路，并用定点振幅放大提高获得学习模型的概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原有量子自动学习（QAL）通过直接更新量子态训练模型，但后选择限制了最终学习模型的获得概率。

Method: 构建后选择无关的QAL算法：将完整训练路径整合为一个相干量子电路，采用定点振幅放大提升目标模型成功概率。

Result: 给出输出误差与学习损失的理论保证，并识别出额外相干资源能够降低失败轨迹重启成本的条件。

Conclusion: 该构造使QAL具备相干实现、明确的学习保证与资源成本，能以高概率获得学习质量可认证、输出误差受控的模型。

Abstract: Quantum automated learning (QAL) trains quantum models by directly updating quantum states, offering an approach to quantum machine learning that avoids optimizing variational circuit parameters. However, post-selection constrains the probability of obtaining the final learned model. We develop a post-selection-free QAL algorithm that organizes a complete training path into a coherent quantum circuit and uses fixed-point amplitude amplification to increase the probability of obtaining the learned model. We provide theoretical guarantees on output error and learning loss, and identify conditions under which additional coherent resources reduce the cost of restarting failed trajectories. The construction gives QAL a coherent implementation with explicit learning guarantees and resource costs, enabling a model with certified learning quality and controlled output error to be obtained with high probability.

</details>


### [94] [Measurement Complexity of Quantum Compressed Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08234)
*Jianyong Hu,Wei Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文从信息论与量子物理角度建立了量子压缩感知（QCS）的测量复杂度下界，指出量子并行虽可实现基对齐与索引标记，但有效索引样本复杂度仍需 Ω(K) 乃至 Θ(K ln K + K/ε²)，并未突破经典下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典压缩感知在非自适应测量下存在 M = Ω(K log(N/K)) 的测量下界，而近期量子压缩感知实验报告了低于该经典下界的测量次数。作者旨在厘清这一现象的本质，从信息论与量子物理角度给出 QCS 测量复杂度的严格下界，说明量子并行带来的真实收益与极限。

Method: 利用量子并行性，通过酉域对齐演化作用于全部 N 个基态的叠加态，将稀疏基一对一映射到测量基，使非零分量位置由测量索引标签显式携带；在独立单索引投影测量框架下，假设支撑未知、非零概率满足 pn = Θ(1/K)，分析以固定成功概率精确恢复支撑所需的采样覆盖要求，并进一步对每个非零幅度系数施加逐坐标相对均方根误差 ε 的约束，推导最优有效索引样本复杂度。

Result: 若仅需 K 个非零分量的基本统计信息，总有效索引样本数满足粗略必要条件 Ms = Ω(K)；在支撑未知且要求以固定成功概率精确恢复时，Ms = Θ(K ln K)；若进一步要求每个非零幅度达到逐坐标相对均方根误差 ε，则最优有效索引样本复杂度为 Ms = Θ(K ln K + K/ε²)，固定 ε 时退化为 Θ(K ln K)。多模光子数分辨探测可减少实验轮数，但不降低总有效索引样本复杂度。

Conclusion: QCS 报道的低测量次数源于量子并行带来的基对齐与索引标签机制，而非突破信息论下界；其有效索引样本复杂度仍受 Ω(K) 与 Θ(K ln K + K/ε²) 限制。该结果表明量子并行不仅是量子计算的核心资源，也可能在测量科学中发挥变革性作用。

Abstract: Conventional compressed sensing (CS) has a measurement lower bound of M = Ω(K log(N/K)) under non-adaptive measurements. Recent experiments on quantum compressed sensing (QCS) have reported numbers of measurements below this classical lower bound. In this work, we establish lower bounds on the measurement complexity of QCS from an information-theoretic and quantum-physical perspective. QCS exploits quantum parallelism, enabling a unitary domain-alignment evolution to act on a superposition of all N basis states and map the sparse basis onto the measurement basis in a one-to-one manner. Consequently, the locations of nonzero components are explicitly carried by measurement-index labels. If only basic statistical information about the K nonzero components is required, the total number of effective index samples satisfies the coarse necessary condition Ms = Ω(K). Under independent single-index projective measurements, when the support is unknown, the nonzero probabilities satisfy pn = Θ(1/K), and exact support recovery is required with a fixed success probability, the sampling-coverage requirement leads to Ms = Θ(K ln K). If each nonzero amplitude coefficient is further required to achieve a coordinate-wise relative root-mean-square error ε, the optimal effective index sample complexity is Ms = Θ(K ln K + K/ε2), which reduces to Θ(K ln K) for fixed ε. Multimode photon-number-resolving detection can reduce the number of experimental rounds but not the total effective index sample complexity. These results show that quantum parallelism is not only a core resource for quantum computing but can also play a transformative role in measurement science.

</details>


### [95] [Inertial Counterdiabatic Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08235)
*Julián Ferreiro-Vélez,F. A. Cárdenas-López,F. Motzoi,Xi Chen*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 绝热捷径（CD）设计通常依赖复杂或难以实现的逆向工程，现有变分CD协议（如基于作用量最小化）在能隙闭合等强激发区域抑制非绝热激发的能力有限，需要一种能同时利用时间非局域性与代数非局域性的更高效设计框架。

Method: 将CD设计重新表述为移动框架下的对角化问题：采用Magnus展开构造由可实现的CD拟设生成的变分传播子；在新旋转框架内优化控制场以压制哈密顿量的非对角项；并将CD项定义为抵消相应的惯性贡献。传播子的序贯设计引入时间非局域性，Magnus展开的嵌套对易子自然提供额外控制方向（代数非局域性）。

Result: 在双量子比特系统和一维量子Ising链上进行基准测试，该方法一致优于基于作用量最小化的变分CD协议；性能提升在能隙闭合区域最为显著，表明结合时间与代数非局域性可有效抑制非绝热激发。

Conclusion: 将CD设计视为移动框架对角化问题并借助Magnus展开，可同时利用时间与代数非局域性，显著提升绝热捷径的激发抑制性能，为强耦合或临界区域的高保真量子控制提供了一种有效途径。

Abstract: We introduce an inertial counterdiabatic (CD) protocol that reformulates shortcut-to-adiabaticity design as a moving-frame diagonalization problem. The method employs the Magnus expansion to construct a variational propagator generated by an accessible CD ansatz. In the new rotating frame, the controls are optimized to suppress the off-diagonal Hamiltonian terms, and the CD terms are defined to counteract the corresponding inertial contribution. The propagator generated with the Magnus expansion naturally exploits additional control directions generated through its nested commutator, while the sequential design of the propagator introduces temporal nonlocality into the optimization. We benchmark the method on a two-qubit system and on the one-dimensional quantum Ising chain, where it consistently outperforms variational CD protocols based on action minimization. The largest improvements are observed near gap-closing regions, demonstrating the advantage of combining temporal and algebraic nonlocality for suppressing diabatic excitations.

</details>


### [96] [Multi-time advantage for certifying quantum coherence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08241)
*Karol Sajnok,Fabio Costa*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文定义多时间量子过程的相干性，证明中间时刻可见的相干性可超出所有成对信道所能刻画的范围，并在超导量子处理器上通过Ising模型实验观测到这一多时间相干性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放量子系统中的非经典动力学可能隐藏于时间关联中，而标准的基于态与信道的检测方法无法识别这类相干性，因此需要从多时间过程角度重新定义并量化相干性。

Method: 定义多时间量子过程的相干性；将非相干过程刻画为所有输入输出空间退相位操作的固定点；采用相对熵量化相干性；以Ising模型为验证，使三个成对信道均非相干而完整三时间过程保持相干；在超导量子处理器上使用相干性见证与全过程层析进行观测。

Result: 在Ising模型中，三个成对信道均为非相干，但完整三时间过程仍保持相干；该多时间相干性在超导量子处理器上被实验观测到。

Conclusion: 中间时刻的访问能够揭示所有对应成对信道中不存在的相干性，表明多时间过程相干性是超越态与信道检测框架的非经典资源，并具实验可实现性。

Abstract: Non-classical dynamics in open quantum systems can be hidden in temporal correlations that are invisible to standard state- and channel-based tests. We define coherence for multi-time quantum processes and show that access to intermediate times can reveal coherence absent from all corresponding pairwise channels. Incoherent processes are fixed points of dephasing on all input and output spaces, and their coherence is quantified by relative entropy. As a proof of principle, we use an Ising model in which all three pairwise channels are incoherent while the full three-time process remains coherent. We observe this multi-time coherence on a superconducting quantum processor using a coherence witness and full process tomography.

</details>


### [97] [Pauli Decomposition by Character Theory: A Memory-Bounded Algorithm for Qubits and Qudits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08299)
*Mohammadreza Khellat,Mohammad Masoumi,Saman Nasoori,Soroush Nasoori*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文从阿贝尔移位-特征格上的傅里叶分析视角重构Pauli分解，指出其本质是群FFT（在(ℤ₂)ⁿ上即Walsh-Hadamard变换），并据此实现有内存上界的工具库paulikit。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pauli分解支撑Trotterization、相位估计、稀疏哈密顿量模拟和变分可观测量等任务，但朴素实现代价随比特数指数增长。作者希望从群论与傅里叶分析的角度统一理解并降低该分解的代价，尤其是内存瓶颈。

Method: 利用(ℤ₂)ⁿ的特征理论：特征χ_z(v)=(-1)^{⟨v,z⟩}即对角Pauli串，因此任意对角算子天然已是Pauli分解形式。一般算子只需引入移位：每个X^{⊗x}耦合到特征χ_z=Z^{⊗z}，乘积X^{⊗x}Z^{⊗z}（相差{±1,±i}相位）构成Pauli群，即(ℤ₂)ⁿ×(ℤ₂)ⁿ由该相位群在辛配对下的中心扩张。扩张的截面是自由选择：裸乘积，或在x与z重叠的每个量子比特处插入Y=iXZ的真实算子。将该问题视为阿贝尔移位-特征格上的傅里叶分析（自然的DFT），算法即对应群的FFT——在(ℤ₂)ⁿ上为Walsh-Hadamard变换，代价O(n·4ⁿ)。并将框架推广到(ℤ_p)ⁿ以恢复Heisenberg-Weyl qudit。

Result: paulikit作为该量子比特变换的有界内存实现：峰值内存不必物化稠密的2ⁿ×2ⁿ算子，而已有代码的O(1)额外开销恰恰压在该缓冲区上；该实现在超过十亿个振子项上针对Work-Span与带宽上限进行了验证。

Conclusion: Pauli分解可统一理解为群FFT/DFT，其快速算法由群结构决定（(ℤ₂)ⁿ对应Walsh-Hadamard，代价O(n·4ⁿ)），且该视角不局限于量子比特，可推广至qudit；paulikit验证了内存有界实现可在超大规模下满足计算与带宽约束。

Abstract: Pauli decomposition underpins Trotterization, phase estimation, sparse-Hamiltonian simulation, and variational observables, at exponential naive cost. From the character theory of $(\mathbb{Z}_2)^n$, characters $χ_z(v)=(-1)^{\langle v,z\rangle}$ are the diagonal Pauli strings, so any diagonal operator is already Pauli-decomposed. A general operator still needs the shifts: each $X^{\otimes x}$ couples to a character $χ_z=Z^{\otimes z}$, and the products $X^{\otimes x}Z^{\otimes z}$ -- up to phases in $\{\pm 1,\pm i\}$ -- form the Pauli group, the central extension of $(\mathbb{Z}_2)^n\times(\mathbb{Z}_2)^n$ by that phase group fixed by the symplectic pairing. A section of that extension remains a choice: the bare products $X^{\otimes x}Z^{\otimes z}$, or the genuine operators that insert $Y=iXZ$ at every qubit where $x$ and $z$ overlap. As Fourier analysis on an abelian shift-character lattice (a natural DFT), this formulation is not qubit-bound: $(\mathbb{Z}_p)^n$ recovers Heisenberg-Weyl qudits. The fast algorithm is the FFT for the group at hand; on $(\mathbb{Z}_2)^n$ that FFT is Walsh-Hadamard, at cost $O(n\cdot 4^n)$. paulikit is a memory-bounded realization of that qubit transform: peak usage need not materialize the dense $2^n\times 2^n$ operator, unlike prior codes whose $O(1)$ extra sits on that buffer, checked beyond a billion oscillator terms against Work-Span and bandwidth ceilings.

</details>


### [98] [Space-Time Electron Wave Packets with Tunable Skyrmion Textures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08391)
*Sharoz Schezwen,Yijie Shen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究自由电子时空波包（STWP），通过在动量空间约束电子波函数的时空谱，构建出传播不变且群速度可调的物质波包，并揭示其携带meron与skyrmion两种动量空间拓扑织构及拓扑相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时空波包在光学中已实现可调群速度、无衍射及非局域自旋织构等奇异特性，但在物质波中尚未得到充分研究，因此作者将STWP概念拓展至自由电子物质波体系。

Method: 在动量空间对电子波函数的时空谱施加约束以构造自由电子STWP；利用自由电子的独特色散关系分析其可承载的两种拓扑结构，并通过调控谱几何实现拓扑相变与skyrmion数调节。

Result: 自由电子STWP可携带meron与skyrmion两类动量空间拓扑织构；通过谱几何调控可实现拓扑相变和可调skyrmion数；电子脉冲表现出拓扑鲁棒传播，且每个单电子波函数内编码有相应的实空间skyrmionic期望值织构。

Conclusion: 该工作为拓扑自由电子波包在传感与信息传递中的应用开辟了新平台。

Abstract: Space-Time Wave Packets (STWPs) have enabled numerous nontrivial properties, such as tunable group velocities, nondiffraction and nonlocal spin textures in optical fields. However, this has not been properly studied in matter waves. Here, we investigate free-electron STWPs, which are propagation-invariant and group-velocity-tunable matter wave packets, constructed by constraining the spatiotemporal spectrum of the electron wavefunction in momentum space. Due to the unique dispersion of free-electrons, the electron STWP can carry two different topologies, and host both meron and skyrmion topological textures in momentum space. We demonstrate the topological phase transitions and tunable skyrmion numbers by spectral geometry tuning. We also show the topologically robust propagation of the electron pulses and corresponding real-space skyrmionic expectation value textures encoded within each single electron wavefunction. Our results open a platform for topological free-electron wavepackets uses in sensing and information transfer.

</details>


### [99] [A 3D-Printed GHz Microwave-Resonator Paul Trap for Electron Confinement and Millisecond Spin-Qubit Coherence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08411)
*Niklas V. Lausti,Vineet Kumar,Ivan Hudák,Jiří Hajnyš,Peter Kúš,Radek Plašil,Michal Hejduk*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文报道了一种3D打印的GHz频段微波谐振器Paul阱，可维持电子约束所需的开放几何结构，实测品质因数约1000，FEM模型经实验验证后用于计算囚禁区电磁场并估计电子自旋量子比特相干时间，预测可达约10 ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子约束需要GHz频段的驱动频率，同时还需保留适合激光操作与离子库仑晶体成像的开放几何结构，这对传统Paul阱设计构成挑战；此外，需评估该类系统用于量子信息处理的可行性，尤其是电子自旋量子比特的相干性。

Method: 采用3D打印制造微波谐振器Paul阱；实验表征其品质因数与制造缺陷，并与有限元法（FEM）预测对比验证模型；利用验证后的FEM模型计算囚禁区电磁场分布，结合表面粗糙度测量，建立电子自旋量子比特退相干模型以估计相干时间，假设运动加热由Johnson噪声主导。

Conclusion: 该3D打印微波谐振器Paul阱在保持开放几何结构的同时实现GHz驱动，验证了其用于电子约束与量子信息处理的可行性；所提出的相干时间估计方法具有通用性，可推广应用于任何电子Paul阱。

Abstract: We report a 3D-printed microwave-resonator Paul trap that operates at the GHz frequencies required for electron confinement while preserving an open geometry suitable for laser access and imaging ion Coulomb crystals. The resonator exhibits moderate quality factors of approximately 1000, enabling large confining electric-field amplitudes at low input power, and is in good agreement with finite-element-method (FEM) predictions despite manufacturing imperfections, which we experimentally characterize. The validated FEM model is then used to calculate the electromagnetic fields in the trapping region. These field distributions, together with surface-roughness measurements, are incorporated into a model of electron spin-qubit decoherence to estimate the coherence time. Our analysis predicts that electron spin-qubit coherence times on the order of 10 ms should be achievable under the assumption that motional heating is dominated by Johnson noise, making such systems relevant for quantum information processing. The method of obtaining the coherence time is applicable to any electron Paul trap.

</details>


### [100] [Harnessing Post-selected von Neumann Measurements to Strengthen Entanglement and Quantum Signatures in Coherent States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08438)
*Janarbek Yuanbek,Zelalem Abebe Bekele,Zhi-qiang Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究后选择冯·诺依曼测量对双模纠缠相干态(ECS)非经典性质的调控作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究后选择测量如何作为一种可调手段来改造双模纠缠相干态的非经典特性。

Method: 从条件指针态出发，表征压缩、光子统计、模间关联、纠缠、保真度及后选择概率；并利用中等耦合下的反常弱值进行调控。

Result: 具有中等耦合的反常弱值增强了压缩、反群聚、亚泊松统计、模间关联与两体纠缠；保真度刻画了从弱扰动到强可区分性的过渡。

Conclusion: 后选择是一种可调谐的纠缠相干态工程化方法。

Abstract: We investigate how post-selected von Neumann measurements modify the nonclassical properties of two-mode entangled coherent states (ECSs). From the conditional pointer state, we characterize squeezing, photon statistics, intermode correlations, entanglement, fidelity, and post-selection probability. Anomalous weak values with moderate coupling enhance squeezing, antibunching, sub-Poissonian statistics, intermode correlations, and bipartite entanglement, while fidelity captures the crossover from weak perturbation to strong distinguishability. These results demonstrate post-selection as a tunable approach to engineering ECSs.

</details>


### [101] [Quantifying the fidelity of a quantum memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08445)
*Victor Barizien,Sophie Egelhaaf,Angelo Gelmini Rodriguez,Louis Nicolas,Théo Sanchez Mejia,Mikael Afzelius,Pavel Sekatski,Nicolas Brunner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于量子信道理论的通用框架，用于操作化、高效地认证量子存储器并量化其性能，在固态量子存储器实验中认证出98.6%的平均信道保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子存储器是未来量子网络的关键组件，但对其进行认证既重要又困难，亟需操作化且高效的性能量化方法。

Method: 借助量子信道理论的进展，引入平均信道保真度来量化量子存储器性能，并利用由探针态构成的2-design在实验中精确估计该量；进一步提出多种见证（witnessing）技术来认证该保真度，并据此获取信道维度、量子信息传输容量等性质；讨论场景以prepare-and-measure为主，同时涉及基于纠缠的装置及半器件无关方法。

Result: 在固态量子存储器实验中应用上述方法，认证得到98.6%的平均信道保真度。

Conclusion: 该认证框架可操作、高效，并能推广应用于光纤、换能器和量子门等其他量子器件。

Abstract: Quantum memories are key ingredients for future quantum networks. Developing methods for their certification is therefore an important, yet challenging, problem. In this work, we present a general framework for certifying quantum memories and quantifying their performance in an operational and efficient manner, taking advantage of developments in the theory of quantum channels. We start by giving a pedagogical introduction to these concepts and tools and illustrate their natural application to quantum memories. Specifically, we quantify the fidelity of a quantum memory via the notion of average channel fidelity, which can be estimated precisely in an experiment via a finite set of probe states forming a so-called $2$-design. We then present a number of witnessing techniques for certifying this quantity, and show that it gives access to other important properties of the channel, such as its dimensionality and its capacity to transmit quantum information. We consider mostly a simple prepare-and-measure scenario, but discuss also the case of an entanglement-based setup, as well as a semi-device-independent approach. Finally, we apply these methods to a solid-state quantum memory experiment, certifying an average channel fidelity of 98.6%. Our certification methods can be readily applied to other quantum devices, such as fibers, transducers and gates.

</details>


### [102] [Quantum algorithms for four problems in density peak clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08498)
*Gonçalo Sousa,Duarte Magano*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种量子蒙特卡洛密度估计方法，并基于该方法为完整聚类问题及决策聚类、簇计数、重簇计数三个相关任务设计了量子算法，在数据集相关参数满足一定缩放假设下实现了多项式级量子查询加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚类是无监督学习的基础任务，目标是将未标注数据划分为簇，使相似元素归入同一簇、不同簇的元素相异。密度峰聚类中的密度估计是关键步骤，作者希望借助量子计算加速这一过程及相关聚类任务。

Method: 提出一种用于密度估计的量子蒙特卡洛方法，作为密度峰聚类中关键密度估计步骤的基础；在此基础上构建针对完整聚类问题以及决策聚类、簇计数、重簇计数三类任务的量子算法，并在数据集相关参数缩放的假设下进行量子查询复杂度分析，同时开展数值实验验证假设条件。

Result: 对每个任务均建立了多项式级量子查询加速（在依赖数据集的参数满足特定缩放假设下）；数值实验表明这些条件在若干实际相关场景中能够得到满足。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，通过量子蒙特卡洛密度估计可有效加速密度峰聚类及其相关任务，在合理参数条件下获得相对经典方法的多项式级量子查询优势。

Abstract: Clustering is a fundamental task in unsupervised learning. The goal is to partition unlabeled data into clusters such that similar elements are assigned to the same cluster, while elements in different clusters are dissimilar. We propose a quantum Monte Carlo approach to density estimation, a key routine in density peak clustering. Building on this approach, we develop quantum algorithms for the full clustering problem and three related tasks: decision clustering, cluster counting, and heavy cluster counting. For each one, we establish a polynomial quantum query speedup under assumptions on the scaling of dataset-dependent parameters. Numerical experiments suggest that these conditions are satisfied for several practically relevant settings.

</details>


### [103] [Exact Catalysis Cannot Overcome the Gaussian-Steering Barrier for Remote Wigner Negativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08509)
*Sudip Chakrabarty*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究多副本、辅助系统或催化剂能否在Alice仅限高斯操作时突破高斯steering对远程Wigner负性生成的限制，并提出"steered negativity"这一乘性单调量进行刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯steering是远程生成Wigner负性的阈值：只有当Alice能用高斯测量steer Bob时，Bob的测量才能使Alice持有的共享高斯态部分出现Wigner负性。作者追问多副本、ancillas或catalysts能否在Alice受限为高斯操作时解除这一限制。

Method: 引入steered negativity——一种乘性单调量，其对数对高斯态等于高斯steerability，用于界定Bob能herald出的负性；并针对严格不可steer的高斯态、单轮feed-forward到Alice的高斯幺正情形，分析Wigner负性或steerable催化剂精确返还时的作用。

Result: steered negativity给出Bob可herald负性的上界，排除了副本激活、Bob的ancillas激活以及无自身远程负性的catalysts激活。对严格不可steer的高斯态，在单轮feed-forward到Alice高斯幺正的条件下，即使催化剂为Wigner负或steerable，只要其被精确返还也无法提供帮助。

Conclusion: 高斯steering构成远程Wigner负性的屏障，且该结论超越单副本情形，在引入副本、ancillas与催化剂后依然成立。

Abstract: Gaussian steering sets a sharp threshold for creating Wigner negativity at a distance. A measurement by Bob can make Alice's part of a shared Gaussian state Wigner negative only if Alice can steer him with Gaussian measurements. We ask whether multiple copies, ancillas, or catalysts can lift this requirement when Alice is restricted to Gaussian operations. We introduce the steered negativity, a multiplicative monotone whose logarithm equals the Gaussian steerability for Gaussian states. It bounds the negativity that Bob can herald and rules out activation by copies, by ancillas of Bob, and by catalysts without remote negativity of their own. For a strictly unsteerable Gaussian state and a single round of feed-forward to a Gaussian unitary of Alice, we further prove that even a Wigner-negative or steerable catalyst cannot help if it is returned exactly. Gaussian steering thus remains a barrier to remote Wigner negativity beyond the single-copy setting.

</details>


### [104] [Optimal GHZ extraction from MABK violations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08516)
*Avraham Kreindel,Aryeh Lev Zabokritskiy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文确定了任意多体（≥3）情形下，MABK Bell分数所容许的最小GHZ可提取度：在双可分界与量子最大值之间，精确最小值为从1/2到1的仿射插值，并给出可达态的分类与稳定性结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GHZ可提取度（与GHZ态的最大平方重叠，经分别的局域量子信道）与MABK Bell分数之间存在定量约束关系；此前六方及以上的最低可提取度范围尚未解决，且在不同局域维度（qubit约束）下精确下界与最优态结构不明确。

Method: 对张量积任意局域维度上的正规态及任意二值测量，给出统一自四方起成立的解析证明，得到MABK分数与可提取度间的精确下界；区分一般局域维度与全qubit情形；构造一qutrit加其余qubit的态达到该下界并保持测量固定；在最小局域支撑维数乘积下分类达到下界的态；并以迹范数距离界定超过仿射最小值的可提取度余量，证明指数1/2最优且常数与方数无关（固定内区间）。

Result: 对任意方数>2，介于双可分界与量子最大值之间的精确最小可提取度为从1/2到1的仿射插值；全qubit时最小值严格高于无约束下界（每个内点分数）；一qutrit加qubit可达到下界所有点且测量固定；对≥4方与严格内点分数，完成达到下界态的分类；对≥4方（一qutrit加qubit），当可提取度余量小时，迹范数距离受常数乘余量平方根控制，指数1/2最优，常数在固定归一化分数内区间上与方数无关。

Conclusion: MABK Bell分数对GHZ可提取度的约束在≥4方具有统一的仿射下界，qubit限制抬高该下界；下界可由一qutrit加qubit的最优态达到并可分类；且下界附近的稳定性由平方根型迹范数界刻画，指数最优且常数具方数无关性。

Abstract: We determine the least Greenberger-Horne-Zeilinger (GHZ) extractability compatible with a Mermin-Ardehali-Belinskii-Klyshko (MABK) Bell score for every number of parties greater than two. Extractability is the largest squared overlap with a GHZ state obtainable by separate local quantum channels. Between the biseparable bound and the quantum maximum, the exact minimum is the affine interpolation from one half to one. The bound holds for normal states on tensor products of arbitrary local dimension and arbitrary binary measurements. We settle the previously unresolved range of six or more parties with an analytic proof that is uniform from four parties onward. We also determine the exact minimum when every local system is a qubit, and show that it lies strictly above the unrestricted bound at every interior score. One qutrit and qubits at all remaining parties attain every point of the bound with measurements fixed as the score varies. For at least four parties and strict interior scores, we classify all states attaining the bound with the minimum product of local support dimensions. For at least four parties on one qutrit and qubits elsewhere, we also prove that, when the extractability excess above the affine minimum is small, the trace-norm distance from the attaining mixture, up to local unitaries, is bounded by a constant times the square root of that excess. The exponent $1/2$ is optimal, and the constants are independent of the number of parties on every fixed interior interval of normalized scores.

</details>


### [105] [Universal critical $g$ factor for spin-1/2 Aharonov-Bohm bound states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08521)
*Vinícius Salem,Edilberto O. Silva,Fabiano M. Andrade*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过自伴延拓方法澄清并加强了Hagen关于自旋-1/2 Aharonov-Bohm哈密顿量不存在束缚态的结论：Hagen解对应奇异Zeeman相互作用的一参数自伴延拓族中的特殊成员，微观物理选定的延拓参数在g=2（Dirac值）时恰好为ν=0，且与磁通扇区及正则化半径无关；异常磁矩是奇异通道中产生束缚态的充要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋-1/2 Aharonov-Bohm哈密顿量是否支持束缚态自Hagen得出结论（Dirac粒子不存在此类束缚态）以来一直存在争议。核心问题在于奇异Zeeman相互作用对应的哈密顿量在原点处不自伴，需要选取自伴延拓，而不同延拓成员会导致不同物理结论，因此需要确定微观物理实际选取哪一个延拓成员，从而判定束缚态是否存在及其条件。

Method: 作者将Hagen解识别为描述奇异Zeeman相互作用的一参数自伴延拓族中的某一特定成员，并确定微观物理所选择的成员。具体做法是将延拓参数与有限半径磁通管进行匹配（正则化），通过这一匹配得到延拓参数ν的值，并据此分析束缚态存在性与g因子的关系。

Result: 将延拓参数与有限半径磁通管匹配后，得到在g=2时恰好ν=0，且在每一个磁通扇区中都成立。因此临界g因子是普适的，g_c=2，既不依赖于磁通扇区也不依赖于正则化半径。磁通扇区控制束缚态能量的深度，作者给出了闭式表达式，表明束缚随磁通整数部分的增大而显著加深。

Conclusion: Hagen的结论得到确认并进一步精确化：它并非关于延拓族中任意一个成员的陈述，而是关于当磁矩取Dirac值时物理所选定的那个成员的陈述；异常磁矩是奇异通道中产生束缚态的充要条件。

Abstract: Whether the spin-1/2 Aharonov-Bohm Hamiltonian supports bound states has remained controversial since Hagen concluded that no such states exist for a Dirac particle. Here, we show that Hagen's solution corresponds to a particular member of the one-parameter family of self-adjoint extensions that describe the singular Zeeman interaction, and we identify which member the microscopic physics selects. Matching the extension parameter to a finite-radius flux tube yields $ν=0$ precisely at $g=2$, and it does so in every flux sector. The critical $g$ factor is therefore universal, $g_c=2$, and depends on neither the flux sector nor the regularization radius. The flux sector controls the depth of the bound state energy, and we obtain a closed-form expression showing that binding deepens markedly with the integer part of the flux. Hagen's conclusion is thus confirmed and sharpened: it is not a statement about one arbitrary member of a family of extensions, but about the member that the physics selects when the magnetic moment takes its Dirac value, and an anomalous moment is a necessary and sufficient condition for binding in the singular channel.

</details>


### [106] [Physical Design Automation for Planar Superconducting Quantum Chips](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08580)
*Michael Feldmeier,Marcel Walter,Gerhard B. P. Huber,Anirban Bhattacharjee,Stefan Filipp,Robert Wille*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文针对超导量子芯片布线仍依赖人工、耗时长且现有自动化方法因简化问题而难以达到可制造标准的问题，提出形式化抽象与三阶段设计自动化流程，实现全自动、秒级至分钟级生成符合物理设计规则的可制造布局，并开源为 mqt-scpd 工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导电路是极具前景且可扩展的量子计算平台，工业采用推动量子比特数持续增长，但芯片布局仍主要依靠人工，即使是中等规模设计也需专家耗费数天乃至数周。现有自动化方法通过简化底层问题和放松物理约束来回避该瓶颈，因而无法给出可直接制造的完整结果。为在不做此类妥协的前提下突破可扩展性障碍，作者引入严格的问题建模与自动化流程。

Method: 首先提出一种形式化抽象，将物理器件特性转化为严格的几何问题表述，从而连接物理实现与设计自动化。在此基础上提出三阶段设计自动化流程：几何感知的全局划分、基于 ILP 的端口分配以及分层布线流水线。评估由设计自动化研究者与超导硬件专家组成的跨学科团队完成。

Result: 评估确认该流程可一键生成完整、可制造且符合物理设计规则的布局；全自动运行，耗时在数秒到数分钟之间；对复杂实例的求解速度最高比当前最先进方法快 25 倍。

Conclusion: 所有方法以完全开源工具 mqt-scpd 的形式发布，作为慕尼黑量子工具包（Munich Quantum Toolkit）的一部分，提供了首个可直接使用的端到端平面超导量子芯片设计流程，并显式处理其底层物理特性与要求。

Abstract: Superconducting circuits have emerged as one of the most promising and scalable platforms for quantum com- puting, with substantial industrial adoption driving qubit counts steadily upward. Yet the layout of the corresponding chips is still primarily performed manually, consuming multiple days or weeks of expert effort, even for moderately sized designs. Existing automation approaches sidestep this bottleneck by simplifying the underlying problem and relaxing physical constraints. Therefore, they fall short of fabrication-ready results. To overcome this scalability wall without such compromises, we first introduce a formal abstraction that translates physical device characteristics into a rigorous geometric problem formulation. This abstraction bridges physical realization and design automation. Building upon this abstraction, we then propose a three-stage design automation flow comprising geometry-aware global partitioning, ILP-based port assignment, and a hierarchical routing pipeline. Evaluations conducted by our interdisciplinary team of design au- tomation researchers and superconducting hardware experts con- firm that the resulting flow produces complete, manufacturing- ready layouts that comply with the physical design rules at the push of a button. That is, it operates fully automatically within seconds to a few minutes, and solves complex instances up to 25x faster than the state of the art. All methods are released as the fully open-source tool mqt-scpd as part of the Munich Quantum Toolkit, providing the first readily usable, end- to-end design flow for planar superconducting quantum chips that explicitly addresses the underlying physical characteristics and requirements.

</details>


### [107] [Fault-tolerant resource estimation for ground-state preparation via Lindblad simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08667)
*Marius Bothe,Patrick Schoepf,Nick S. Blunt*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在早期容错量子计算阶段利用Lindblad动力学（单辅助比特方法）制备费米Hubbard模型基态的实际成本，通过严格误差界与电路级模拟对比，发现经验参数可将资源降低数个量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lindblad动力学仿真算法在态制备上的理论潜力已被阐明，但其在早期容错及后续阶段的实用性仍不明确，因此需要评估具体模型（费米Hubbard模型）基态制备的实际成本。

Method: 采用单辅助比特（single-ancilla）方法，针对36格点费米Hubbard模型低能子空间的基态制备，推导含常数前因子的严格误差界，并与电路级模拟得到的经验误差行为和实用收敛参数进行对比。

Result: 经验选取的参数可将所需资源降低数个量级；估计执行一单位时间演化、面向36格点费米Hubbard模型低能子空间需约7.7×10^8个T门。能量跃迁的精确过滤被认定为该成本的主要来源。

Conclusion: Lindblad动力学基态制备在实际早期容错场景下的成本主要由能量跃迁精确过滤决定；理论误差界可能显著高估所需资源，经验调参对降低开销至关重要。

Abstract: Recent advances in algorithms for simulating Lindblad dynamics have clarified their theoretical potential for state preparation, but their practicality in the early fault-tolerant regime and beyond remains uncertain. In this work, we address this by investigating the cost of preparing ground states of the fermionic Hubbard model, following the single-ancilla approach of [Phys. Rev. Research 6, 033147 (2024)]. We derive rigorous error bounds including constant prefactors, and compare to empirical error behavior and practical convergence parameters obtained from circuit-level simulations. We find that empirically chosen parameters can reduce the required resources by orders of magnitude, resulting in an estimated $7.7 \times 10^8$ T gates to perform one unit of time evolution targeting the low-energy subspace of a 36-site fermionic Hubbard model. We identify the accurate filtering of energy transitions as the main source of this cost.

</details>


### [108] [Entanglement Sudden Death: Eberly's Work and the Limits of Local Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08710)
*Abhaya S. Hegde,Songbo Xie*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文回顾了纠缠突然死亡(ESD)从两体到真正多体纠缠的发展，并以双量子比特独立阻尼与广义GHZ态去极化为例，揭示多体纠缠寿命不由局部动力学决定，而取决于联合量子态的演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梳理ESD从两体起源到真正多体纠缠的研究脉络，并探究多体纠缠的寿命究竟由局部动力学还是联合量子态演化决定。

Method: 先用两个独立阻尼量子比特说明ESD的典型机制（concurrence有限时间归零，而布居与相干仍非零）；再研究去极化下广义GHZ态的多体纠缠，比较全局与独立局部去极化动力学（二者对单个量子比特约化演化相同）对真正多体纠缠突然死亡时间的影响，并分析N量子比特GHZ态的标度行为。

Result: 全局与独立局部去极化可给出每个量子比特相同的约化演化，但真正多体纠缠突然死亡时间不同，其排序可随初始态反转；对N量子比特GHZ态，独立局部去极化下ESD时间按1/N标度，而全局去极化下在热力学极限趋近有限值，区别随N渐近增强。

Conclusion: 多体纠缠的寿命不由局部动力学固定，而取决于联合量子态的演化，呼应了Eberly工作的核心启示。

Abstract: Entanglement sudden death (ESD), explored through a series of works by Yu and Eberly, supports the notion of entanglement vanishing abruptly and completely at a finite time, even when the underlying open-system dynamics remain smooth. In this article, we revisit the development of ESD from bipartite origins to genuine multipartite entanglement. We first illustrate the canonical mechanism of ESD with two independently damped qubits, for which the concurrence vanishes at a finite time while populations and coherences remain nonzero. We next present our results on multipartite entanglement for generalized GHZ states under depolarization. Global and independent local depolarizing dynamics can be chosen to produce identical reduced evolution for every individual qubit, yet they yield different genuine-multipartite-entanglement sudden death times, whose ordering can reverse with the initial state. For N-qubit GHZ states, this distinction becomes asymptotically stronger since the ESD time scales as 1/N under independent local depolarization, whereas under global depolarization it approaches a finite value in the thermodynamic limit. Echoing a central lesson of Prof. Eberly's work, we report the lifetime of multipartite entanglement is not fixed by the local dynamics, but rather depends on the evolution of the joint quantum state.

</details>


### [109] [Quantum Algorithms for Multivariable Polynomial Transformations: From Efficient Synthesis to Quantum Channel Transformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08714)
*Zheyu Shen,Yusen Wu,Xiao Yuan,Xiao-Ming Zhang,Yukun Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了非交换矩阵多变量多项式变换的完整构造理论，在联合块访问模型下以O(D/√τ)查询复杂度（行块访问下精确D次）合成任意在指定矩阵域上收缩的度-D多项式P，并推广至量子信道变换与因果Choi数据指定的固定阶量子梳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单变量多项式合成（量子信号处理、奇异值变换）已有成熟理论，查询复杂度基本由次数决定；但非交换矩阵的多变量变换缺乏可比的合成理论，本文旨在填补这一空白，并进一步扩展到信道与高阶量子信息处理。

Method: 采用联合块访问（joint block access）模型；核心是有限算法化Schur–Agler定理：系数递推定义一个多项式维数的连续空间，支持在指定域上收缩性的完整半正定证书，分解该证书得到收缩实现，从而构造查询算法；对信道情形使用相干Kraus访问K={K_a}，构造完全正操作；对信道级变换利用因果Choi数据合成固定阶量子梳。

Result: 给定度-D多项式P的紧有限状态描述与0<τ<1，以(1+τ)最优归一化用O(D/√τ)查询合成P，行块访问下精确D次，达到次数下界；完整电路实现可经典多项式时间计算，对精度具多对数依赖；可合成任意有限联合收缩的非交换多项式映射族K↦{F_b(K)}_b为完全正操作，允许Kraus历史间的相干干涉。

Conclusion: 多变量逼近可作为多算子量子算法与高阶量子信息处理的统一语言，所提出的框架将矩阵变换的多项式合成提升至量子信道与因果结构层面，指向更广泛的研究纲领。

Abstract: Polynomial transformations are basic primitives in quantum algorithms: quantum signal processing and singular value transformation compile univariate polynomials into circuits with query complexity essentially set by degree. Multivariable transformations of noncommuting matrices, however, lack a comparable synthesis theory. We develop a complete constructive theory under joint block access for all polynomials contractive on the prescribed matrix domain. Given a compact finite-state description of a degree-$D$ polynomial $P$ and $0<τ<1$, we synthesize $P$ with $(1+τ)$-optimal normalization using $O(D/\sqrtτ)$ queries, and exactly $D$ under row-block access, matching a degree lower bound. The complete circuit realization is classically computable in polynomial time with polylogarithmic dependence on accuracy. The key to our construction is a finite algorithmic Schur--Agler theorem: the coefficient recurrence defines a polynomial-dimensional continuation space supporting a complete semidefinite certificate for contractivity on the prescribed domain. Factoring this certificate yields the contractive realization underlying the query algorithm.
  Beyond matrix transformations, our framework lifts multivariable polynomial synthesis to quantum channel transformations. Given coherent Kraus access $K=\{K_a\}_a$, we synthesize any finite jointly contractive family of noncommutative polynomial maps $K\mapsto\{F_b(K)\}_b$ as completely positive operations, allowing coherent interference among Kraus histories. Furthermore, channel-level transformations specified by causal Choi data can be synthesized explicitly as fixed-order quantum combs. Collectively, these results point to a broader program: multivariable approximation as a language for multi-operator quantum algorithms and higher-order quantum information processing.

</details>


### [110] [Efficient Learning of Structured Fermionic States under General Quadratic Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08723)
*Erfan Amidi,Ali Asadian,Ali Hamed Moosavian*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明：由不相交分支、固定粒子数块制备的未知纯费米子态，在含对产生与湮灭的一般二次演化后仍可重构；在每块粒子数上限固定时，自适应/非自适应单拷贝高斯测量能以系统尺寸与逆不忠实度的多项式资源，给出具有指定保真度与置信度的经典输入与电路描述，其中自适应方案拷贝保证更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究未知纯费米子态在一般二次（高斯）演化下的可重构性，尤其是态由不连通的固定粒子数分块制备、且演化包含粒子对产生与湮灭的情形，并希望在较弱假设下实现高效、可验证的重构。

Method: 采用单拷贝高斯测量（自适应或非自适应）；利用连通四阶关联与协变相容的交换子（commutant）识别隐藏分块；局域态通过新测量或预定有界度矩表恢复；给出稳定分支恢复以得到有效制备描述；并构造认证变体，在承诺族内存在被动制备时返回该制备。

Result: 在每块粒子数上限固定时，自适应或非自适应单拷贝高斯测量均可用多项式资源（系统尺寸与逆不忠实度）给出满足指定保真度与置信度的经典输入及电路描述，自适应过程拷贝保证更强；分支恢复稳定且可给出有效制备描述；不要求粒子数守恒或分块同质性，也不假设分支权重下界或协方差间隙；在配对魔法态的全高斯轨道上，仅四阶测量即足够，并给出将可观测误差转化为态误差的显式界。

Conclusion: 未知纯费米子态在一般二次演化后可被高效重构；所提方法在更弱假设下工作，并可认证地返回被动制备；特定轨道上四阶测量已充分。

Abstract: We prove that unknown pure fermionic states prepared from disjoint-branch fixed-particle-number blocks can be reconstructed after general quadratic evolution, including pair creation and annihilation. For a fixed particle cap per block, adaptive or nonadaptive single-copy Gaussian measurements yield classical input and circuit descriptions with prescribed fidelity and confidence, using polynomial resources in system size and inverse infidelity. The adaptive procedure has the sharper copy guarantee. Connected quartic correlations and a covariance-compatible commutant identify the hidden blocks; local states are recovered from fresh measurements or a predetermined bounded-degree moment table. Stable branch recovery gives valid preparation descriptions. Neither particle-number conservation nor block homogeneity is required; no lower bound on branch weights or covariance gaps is assumed. A certified variant returns a passive preparation whenever one exists within the promised family, without advance membership information. On the full Gaussian orbit of paired magic states, quartic measurements alone suffice, with an explicit bound converting observable error into state error.

</details>


### [111] [No-Disturbance-without-Uncertainty generates the Quantum set in the simplest Bell scenario](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08749)
*Ravishankar Ramanathan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将一类满足"共同重叠无扰-无不确定"条件的无信号行为的凸包，精确刻画为有限维量子集，覆盖一方两二值测量、另一方任意有限设置与结果的二体Bell场景，并由此给出线性Bell表达式优化的简化算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用量子信息论约束刻画量子非局域关联集合是量子基础的核心问题，但量子集一般难以从原理层面刻画，且带局部边缘项的完整量子行为集（如八维情形）此前缺乏精确刻画。

Method: 在二体Bell实验中引入"共同重叠无扰-无不确定"条件，取满足该条件的无信号行为的凸包；通过从条件概率与无信号性直接构造量子实现来证明该凸包等于有限维量子集；并将线性Bell表达式的优化约化为一族二阶锥规划的单参数最大化。

Result: 在"一方两二值测量、另一方任意有限数量设置且每设置任意有限结果"的场景下，该凸包恰等于有限维量子集；在最简二值输入-输出场景中完整刻画了含局部边缘项的八维量子行为集；任意线性Bell表达式的优化可化为一参数族二阶锥规划的最大化。

Conclusion: 局部不确定-扰动约束经由无信号性与共享经典随机性，与量子非局域性建立了联系，为量子关联集提供了信息论式的原理刻画及可计算的优化途径。

Abstract: Characterizing the set of quantum nonlocal correlations through information-theoretic constraints is a central problem in quantum foundations. We show that the convex hull of no-signalling behaviors in a bipartite Bell experiment satisfying a common-overlap no-disturbance-without-uncertainty condition is exactly the finite-dimensional quantum set whenever one party has two binary measurements and the other has an arbitrary finite number of settings, each with an arbitrary finite number of outcomes. In the simplest binary input-output Bell scenario, this characterizes the full eight-dimensional set of quantum behaviors, including the local marginals. The proof constructs a quantum realization directly from the conditional probabilities and no-signalling. The characterization also reduces optimization of any linear Bell expression in these scenarios to maximization over a one-parameter family of second-order cone programs. The results establish a connection between local uncertainty-disturbance constraints and quantum nonlocality, mediated by no-signalling and shared classical randomness.

</details>


### [112] [Complexity of self-consistent entanglement certification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08757)
*Yujie Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究了基于广义非语境性（generalized noncontextuality）的纠缠认证协议在有限实验中的复杂度，给出了达到纠缠分辨率γ所需的局部效应数及其标度，以及认证所需的样本复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作（Phys. Rev. X 16, 031057 (2026)）提出了基于广义非语境性的自洽纠缠认证方法，无需预先表征测量设备，且可认证贝尔电路中的所有纠缠态。但其在有限实验条件下的资源复杂度（所需测量效应数和样本量）尚不清楚，本文旨在量化该协议的复杂度。

Method: 在固定局域维度d下，分析达到基于迹距离的纠缠分辨率γ所需的不同局部效应数，分别考察测量可针对目标态定制的情形与独立Haar随机投影测量的情形；进一步在已知精确操作恒等式的前提下，分析以错误概率不超过δ进行认证所需的样本复杂度。

Result: 最优所需的不同局部效应数为Θ_d(γ^{-(d-1)})，即使测量针对目标态定制该标度也是必要的；独立Haar随机投影测量则需要Θ_d(γ^{-(d-1)}log(1/γ))个效应。在精确操作恒等式已知时，认证所需的目标态副本数为Θ_d(γ^{-2}log(1/δ))，且该数量是充分必要的。

Conclusion: 本文刻画了广义非语境性纠缠认证协议在有限实验中的资源复杂度。当操作恒等式需从同一有限目标态数据中推断时，最优样本复杂度仍是一个开放问题。

Abstract: In recent work Phys. Rev. X 16, 031057 (2026), we proposed a self-consistent approach to entanglement certification based on generalized noncontextuality. It requires no prior characterization of the measurement devices and, given access to all local measurements, can certify every entangled state in a Bell circuit. Here, we study the complexity of this protocol in a finite experiment. At fixed local dimension $d$, we show that the optimal number of distinct local effects needed to reach a trace-distance \textit{entanglement resolution} $γ$ is $Θ_d(γ^{-(d-1)})$. This scaling is necessary even when the measurements are tailored to the target state. Independent Haar-random projective measurements instead require $Θ_d(γ^{-(d-1)}\log(1/γ))$ effects. Finally, we show that, once the exact operational identities are known, $Θ_d(γ^{-2}\log(1/δ))$ copies of the target state are necessary and sufficient for certification with error probability at most $δ$. Determining the optimal sample complexity when those identities must instead be inferred from the same finite target-state data remains open.

</details>


### [113] [Energy-constrained two-way capacity bounds for noisy Gaussian channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08786)
*Stefano Pirandola*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对存在额外噪声的能量受限玻色通信，推导出一个统一的适应性弱逆定理界，适用于热衰减、含噪放大和加性高斯噪声信道，并约束多类双向容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能量受限玻色通信在存在额外噪声时，可达速率与逆定理界之间长期存在差距；现有有限能量逆定理方法多局限于量子极限（无额外噪声）情形，缺乏对含噪高斯信道的统一能量依赖基准。

Method: 在无条件平均发射光子数约束下，推导统一的自适应弱逆（weak-converse）界，覆盖热衰减、含噪放大与加性高斯噪声三类信道；该界适用于带量子存储的任意自适应协议，并约束双向量子、纠缠分发、私密与密钥容量；通过高斯squashed-entanglement界、PLOB界及hashing速率的对比进行评估。

Result: 该界在纠缠破坏区域整体消失，并在无穷能量下趋近相应的PLOB界；在有限能量下，于相关参数区域改进了已评估的高斯squashed-entanglement界与PLOB界，与hashing速率的比较量化了剩余的可达性差距。

Conclusion: 该结果为含噪玻色高斯信道提供了统一的能量依赖基准，并将有限能量逆定理方法从量子极限推广至更一般的额外噪声设定。

Abstract: A gap between achievable rates and converse bounds persists for energy-constrained bosonic communication in the presence of excess noise. We derive a unified adaptive weak-converse bound for thermal attenuation, noisy amplification, and additive Gaussian noise under an unconditional mean transmitted-photon-number constraint. The bound applies to arbitrary adaptive protocols with quantum memories and constrains the two-way quantum, entanglement-distribution, private, and secret-key capacities. It vanishes throughout the entanglement-breaking region and approaches the corresponding PLOB bound at infinite energy. At finite energy, it improves the evaluated Gaussian squashed-entanglement and PLOB bounds in relevant parameter regimes, while comparison with hashing rates quantifies the remaining gap to achievability. The result provides a common energy-dependent benchmark for noisy bosonic Gaussian channels and extends finite-energy converse methods beyond the quantum-limited setting.

</details>
